Gradient: For fixed p, c_p = ∑_{i,j: i-j = p-(n-1)} (h_i - h_i h_j).
For variable a:
As i=a: contributions for all j with k=a-j => p=n-1+a-j. Derivative 1 - h_j.
As j=a: contributions for all i with k=i-a => p=n-1+i-a. Derivative -h_i.
So grad[a] = ∑_j w_{n-1+a-j}(1-h_j) - ∑_i w_{n-1+i-a} h_i.
Let q index. Define W[q]=p_q. For a fixed a:

`project_feasible(h, n)`:

- adjust sum to target using waterfilling: solve threshold such that sum clip(h - τ)=target? This is more stable than previous iterative room. But target may be integer/half. The set of functions with box and sum is convex; projection in L2 is soft threshold: find t with ∑ clip(h_i - t,0,1)=target. We can implement bisection over t ∈ [min-? max]? For h_i, solving ∑ (h_i - t)_+ clipped = target. Since target within [0,n], t in [min(h),max(h)] maybe. Use binary search 60 iters. This gives nearest under L2 to h. Might be better than previous preserving shape. But if h values all 0/1 and target equal, unchanged. Implementation:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
 target=n/2.0
 if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
 lo,hi = float(h.min())-1e-12, float(h.max())+1e-12
 for _ in range(50):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2
   s=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0,1))
   if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
   if hi-lo<1e-13: break
 t=(lo+hi)/2
 return np.clip(h-t,0,1)
```
But if h-t projection not exact sum? Bisection should. For target not integer, continuous t. This projection changes all values by same offset, unlike previous scaled room. Could be okay. But if h near bounds, it zeros/increments. Good.

But exact CD with projection after each pass: A single variable update changes sum; L2 projection shifts all, not ideal but fine. We can instead not project after each variable but after each pass, and when changing variable, fix by adjusting another variable? Previous okay.

FW algorithm details:
```
def fw_optimize(h,n,mode,alpha,T,max_iter,deadline):
  h=h.copy()
  val=eval_obj(...)
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time>... break
    c = compute_c(h,n)
    W = get_weights(c,mode,alpha,T)
    if np.sum(W) <= 0: break
    W = W / W.sum()  # for direction only? For gradient direction, scaling doesn't matter.
    g = linear_weighted_gradient(c,h,n,W)
    # optional: combine with gradient? s linear min
    s = linear_min(g,n)
    # line search steps: start with maybe 2/(it+2) capped at 0.3, also 1.0? Use sequence [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
    best=None; bestv=val
    for eta in steps:
       hn = (1-eta)*h + eta*s
       if not feasible due numerical, project
       v=eval_obj(hn)
       if v<bestv: bestv=v; best=hn; break? or continue to find smaller? use break (Armijo not necessary)
    if best is None:
       # try moving opposite? maybe random perturb
       h = project(h + rng.normal(0,0.01,n))
       continue
    h=best
  return h
```

```
def solve_1d(c, h, p, n):
    L = 2*n - 1
    h0 = h[p]
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1-1e-14:
        return h0
    q1s = n - 1 - p
    q1e = n - 1 + p
    len1 = q1e - q1s + 1  # 2p+1
    q2s = p
    q2e = p + n - 1
    len2 = q2e - q2s + 1  # n-p
    start = min(q1s, q2s)
    end = max(q1e, q2e)
    seg_len = end - start + 1
    a = np.zeros(seg_len)
    rel1 = q1s - start
    a[rel1:rel1+len1] += 1.0 - h[:p+1]
    rel2 = q2s - start
    a[rel2:rel2+len2] -= h[n-p:n][::-1]
    cseg = c[start:end+1]
    center_rel = n - 1 - start
    has_center = 0 <= center_rel < seg_len
    # cseg and a views? a copy; cseg view.
    # zero constraints
    zero = np.abs(a) <= 1e-14
    L0 = 0.0
    if np.any(zero): L0 = float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
    Lhi = float(np.max(cseg))
    # include global outside? if L0 global max? Lhi maybe less than outside but objective max includes outside. Need L lower bounded by outside max if optimizing variable cannot affect it. Use compute outside to tighten.
    # Instead set Lhi = max(float(np.max(c)), Lhi) for HI, L0_out = max(c[:start], c[end+1:]) maybe.
```

```
def feasible(L):
  if L < L0 - 1e-12: return False
  lox=0.0; hix=1.0
  if has_pos:
     val = h0 + float(np.min((L-cpos)/apos))
     if val < hix: hix=val
  if has_neg:
     val = h0 + float(np.max((L-cneg)/aneg))
     if val > lox: lox=val
  if lox > hix + 1e-13: return False
  if has_center:
     A=L-center_old+h0-h0*h0
     if A < -1e-12: return False
     if A < 0.25 - 1e-12:
        disc=1-4*A
        sqrt=np.sqrt(disc if disc>0 else 0)
        r1=(1-sqrt)/2; r2=(1+sqrt)/2
        # infeasible if entire [lox,hix] inside forbidden (r1,r2)
        if lox >= r1 - 1e-12 and hix <= r2 + 1e-12:
            return False
  return True
```

Rectangular/sorted linear minimizer:
```
def linear_min(g, n):
    idx = np.argsort(g)
    s = np.zeros(n)
    target = n/2.0
    k = int(target)
    s[idx[:k]] = 1.0
    rem = target - k
    if rem > 1e-12:
        s[idx[k]] = rem
    return s
```
If target integer, no rem. If n odd target .5, k=floor, rem 0.5. Good. But g is gradient of objective; We minimize g·s. Since objective may be nonconvex but surrogate. If g has negative values, sorted. This gives s in K.

    h = np.array(h, copy=True)
    val = eval_mode(h,n,mode,alpha)
    best = h.copy(); best_val=val
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time > deadline: break
       cn, w = weights...
       if sum w <=0: break
       w_norm = w / (np.sum(w) + 1e-300)
       g = linear_weighted_gradient(cn? Wait gradient formula uses weights on c. If w is for cn objective, weights for c same up to scalar; gradient direction same. Use w.
       s = linear_min(g,n)
       # candidate steps: [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]
       # To avoid irreversible bad jumps, maybe max step 0.3 first? For exploration, include 1.0.
       improved=False
       for eta in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.01):

           # due numerical, clip sum? It should sum target; but clip

           if abs(hn.sum()-n/2) > 1e-8: hn=project_feasible(hn,n)
           v = eval_mode(hn,n,mode,alpha)
           if v < best_val - 1e-12:

       if not improved:
           if it % 20 == 19:

              vp=eval_mode(...)

```

Detailed `exact_cd` pseudo:
```
def exact_cd(h, n, max_passes, deadline):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = corr(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        moved = False
        # order: alternate, maybe random? Use pass number to choose. n order list? To avoid memory, loop range or reversed.
        if it % 2 == 0:
            order = range(n)
        else:
            order = range(n-1,-1,-1)
        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            res = solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
            if res is None: continue
            x, start, a, end, has_center = res
            old = h[p]
            if abs(x-old) > 1e-12:
                delta = x-old
                c[start:end+1] += delta * a
                if has_center:
                    c[n-1] -= delta*delta
                h[p] = x
                moved = True
        if not moved:
            break
        h = project_feasible(h,n)
        # check sum
        c = corr(h)
        cur = compute_c5(h,n)
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur; best_h = h.copy()
        # optional if cur increased a lot? continue anyway
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `solve_1d` returns a built from old h. If `moved` true and h changes, c update uses a. After pass, project and c recompute. Good.
`max_passes` can be high. `solve_1d` uses `c` current within pass. It doesn't update outside max after each variable? It recomputes outside max via c. Good.

Potential problem: If we project and recompute c at each pass, the `exact_cd` best_h may be from before projection? It returns after pass, sum feasible. Good.

`solve_1d` returns a arrays. We need ensure `a` not a view. Good.

Let's implement solve_1d robustly and performance.

```
def solve_1d(c,h,p,n):
    L=2*n-1
    h0=h[p]
    q1s=n-1-p; q1e=n-1+p; len1=q1e-q1s+1
    q2s=p; q2e=p+n-1; len2=q2e-q2s+1
    start = q1s if q1s < q2s else q2s
    end = q1e if q1e > q2e else q2e
    seg_len = end-start+1
    a = np.zeros(seg_len, dtype=float)
    rel1 = q1s-start
    a[rel1:rel1+len1] += 1.0 - h[:p+1]
    rel2 = q2s-start
    a[rel2:rel2+len2] -= h[n-p:n][::-1]
    # outside max
    outside = 0.0
    if start > 0:
       outside = float(np.max(c[:start]))
    if end + 1 < L:
       v = float(np.max(c[end+1:]))
       if v > outside: outside = v
    cseg = c[start:end+1]
    # if all a zero? no.
    zero = np.abs(a) <= 1e-14
    L_low = outside
    if np.any(zero):
       vz = float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
       if vz > L_low: L_low = vz
    L_high = float(np.max(cseg))
    if outside > L_high: L_high = outside
    if L_high <= L_low + 1e-15:
       # no room, return h0 maybe still bounds?
       return (h0, start, a, end, (n-1)>=start and (n-1)<=end)
    center_rel = n-1-start
    has_center = 0 <= center_rel < seg_len
    center_old = float(c[n-1]) if has_center else 0.0
    pos = a > 1e-13
    neg = a < -1e-13
    has_pos = bool(np.any(pos)); has_neg=bool(np.any(neg))
    if has_pos:
       cpos=cseg[pos]; apos=a[pos]
    if has_neg: cneg=cseg[neg]; aneg=a[neg]
    eps=1e-14
    # To speed, if no pos/neg only zero? Then any x? but center affects.
    def feasible? Can't closure. We can inline in loop? But no closures allowed; so write feasibility code inside loop manually, cannot define nested. Need avoid function. In top-level, can't have nested. So do loop with inline.
```
No closures/lambdas. We can't define `feasible` inside solve. We'll write bisection loop with repeated code. Could make top-level helper `feasible_1d(L, L_low, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, h0, center_old, has_center,...)` but that is top-level okay. It can return bool. That's fine. The helper uses arrays. No closures.
```
def feasible_1d(L, L_low, cpos, apos, cneg, aneg, has_pos, has_neg, h0, center_old, has_center):
    if L < L_low - 1e-12: return False
    lox=0.0; hix=1.0
    if has_pos:
       val = h0 + float(np.min((L - cpos)/apos))
       if val < hix: hix = val
    if has_neg:
       val = h0 + float(np.max((L - cneg)/aneg))
       if val > lox: lox = val
    if lox > hix + 1e-12: return False
    if has_center:
       A = L - center_old + h0 - h0*h0
       if A < -1e-12: return False
       if A < 0.25 - 1e-12:
          disc=1.0-4.0*A
          if disc < 0: disc=0.0
          r1=(1.0-math.sqrt(disc))*0.5
          r2=(1.0+math.sqrt(disc))*0.5
          if lox >= r1 - 1e-12 and hix <= r2 + 1e-12:
             return False
    return True
```
Need import math.

But if `apos` values negative? pos mask ensures >. Division maybe overflow if apos tiny; tolerance 1e-13. Good. For has_pos arrays, if cpos huge, val maybe >; ok.

Bisection:
```
lo=L_low; hi=L_high
for _ in range(40):
    if hi-lo < 1e-10: break
    mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
    if feasible_1d(mid,...): hi=mid
    else: lo=mid

def active_cd(h,n,active_frac, deadline, max_passes):

```
def cd_local(h,n,max_passes,deadline,frac=1.0,alpha=20.0):
  h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
  c=corr(h)
  best_c=compute_c5; best_h=h.copy()
  nsel=max(1,int(n*frac)) if frac<1 else n
  for it in range(max_passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    if frac<1:
       cn=c*(2.0/n)
       m=cn.max(); w=np.exp(alpha*(cn-m)); w/=w.sum()
       g=linear_weighted_gradient(c,h,n,w)
       abs_g=np.abs(g)
       # For speed, top indices
       if nsel < n:
          idx = np.argpartition(abs_g, n-nsel)[-nsel:]
          idx = idx[np.argsort(abs_g[idx])[::-1]]  # optional
       else:
          idx = np.arange(n)
    else:
       idx = np.arange(n) if it%2==0 else np.arange(n-1,-1,-1)
    moved=0
    for p in idx:
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # optional skip if abs(g[p]) small? For frac<1 all top. For full, maybe if h close 0/1? not.
       sol=solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
       if sol is None: continue
       x,start,a,end,has_center=sol
       old=h[p]
       if abs(x-old)>1e-11:
          delta=x-old
          c[start:end+1]+=delta*a
          if has_center: c[n-1]-=delta*delta
          h[p]=x
          moved+=1
    if moved==0: break
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=corr(h)
    cur=compute_c5(h,n)
    if cur<best_c:
       best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    # if cur significantly worse than best, maybe rollback? Could happen due activation selection? But each selected update exactly nonincreases current c? Actually 1D solver finds x feasible at L minimal ≤ outside? It should not increase max for current fixed others, but after multiple updates max may not increase? Since each update chosen to minimize max with current others, the new max ≤ old max. However projection can increase. It might increase but maybe not too much. We can if cur > best_c + 0.005: h=best_h.copy(); c=corr(h); continue? This prevents drift. But projection often needed. Use if cur > best_c + 1e-4? To be safe, if cur > best_c*1.01+1e-4, rollback to best_h. But could prevent escape; active CD should be monotonic before projection. Projection small; if it worsens, rollback. We can add: if cur > best_c + 1e-5 + 0.001? Hmm. Since best_c lower, any improvement needed. We can keep best_h and use it for next pass if current worse: `if cur > best_c + 1e-7: h=best_h.copy(); c=corr(h)` to avoid compounding bad. But this may stall. Use a small tolerance 1e-5. Exact updates should improve or equal before projection; projection may worsen by ~1e-3. If so, maybe continue from current not best to settle. Hard.
```

SLSQP objective penalty:
```
def obj_pen(x,n,mode,alpha,pen):
    return objective_value(x,n,mode,alpha) + pen*(x.sum()-0.5*n)**2
def grad_pen(x,n,mode,alpha,pen):
    g=smooth_gradient(x,n,mode,alpha)
    return g + (2.0*pen*(x.sum()-0.5*n))*np.ones(n)
```
`np.ones(n)` allocates; okay. But inside minimize many calls; could use `g + factor` broadcasts without array: `return g + factor`. Yes.

Minimize:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,mode,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    h0=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    pen=1e5
    try:
       res=minimize(obj_pen,h0,args=(n,mode,alpha,pen),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-16})
       h1=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except: return h0,objective
    return h1, compute_c5
```
But `objective_value` uses `corr` and alpha. For SLSQP line search, pen could dominate, and x may become infeasible sum; okay.

def feasible_1d(L,L_low,apos_inv,cpos_a,aneg_inv,cneg_a,has_pos,has_neg,h0,center_old,has_center):
   if L < L_low - 1e-10: return False
   lox=0; hix=1
   if has_pos:
      v = h0 + float(np.min(L*apos_inv - cpos_a))
      if v < hix: hix=v
   if has_neg:
      v = h0 + float(np.max(L*aneg_inv - cneg_a))
      if v > lox: lox=v
   if lox > hix + 1e-10: return False
   if has_center:
      A = L - center_old + h0 - h0*h0
      if A < -1e-10: return False
      if A < 0.25 - 1e-10:
         disc=1-4*A
         if disc<0: root? if A slightly >0.25? handled.
         sqrt=math.sqrt(disc if disc>0 else 0.0)
         r1=0.5*(1-sqrt); r2=0.5*(1+sqrt)
         if lox > r1 + 1e-10 and hix < r2 - 1e-10:
            return False
   return True
```

def interpolate_h(h, n_new):
   n=len(h); x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n); x2=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)
   return project_feasible(np.interp(x2,x1,h),n_new)

Detailed solve code:
```
def solve_1d(c,h,p,n):
    L=2*n-1
    h0=h[p]
    q1s=...
    start=...
    a=np.zeros(seg_len)
    a[rel1:rel1+len1] += 1.0 - h[:p+1]
    a[rel2:rel2+len2] -= h[n-p:n][::-1]
    outside=... max slices
    cseg=c[start:end+1]
    L_high=float(np.max(cseg))
    if outside>L_high: L_high=outside
    center_rel=n-1-start
    a_solve=a.copy()
    a_solve[center_rel]=0.0
    zero=np.abs(a_solve)<=1e-13
    L_low=outside
    if np.any(zero):
       vz=float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
       if vz>L_low: L_low=vz
    pos=a_solve>1e-13; neg=a_solve<-1e-13
    has_pos=...
    if has_pos:
       cpos=cseg[pos]; apos=a_solve[pos]; apos_inv=1/apos; cpos_a=cpos*apos_inv
    ...
    if L_high <= L_low + 1e-12:
       return (h0,start,a,end,False) # has center update? If no move, not needed. Return False to skip? exact loop checks x==old. has_center not used if no update.
    # bisection
    lo,hi=L_low,L_high
    center_old=float(c[n-1])
    for _ in range(25): ... feasible_1d(hi? etc)
    if hi >= L_high - 1e-9:
       return (h0,start,a,end,False)
    # pick x using hi
    L=hi + 1e-10
    lox=0; hix=1
    if has_pos: hix=min(hix, h0+np.min(L*apos_inv-cpos_a))
    if has_neg: lox=max(lox, h0+np.max(L*aneg_inv-cneg_a))
    lox=max(0,lox); hix=min(1,hix)
    has_root=False; r1=r2=0
    A=L-center_old+h0-h0*h0
    if A < -1e-10: return h0...
    if A < 0.25 - 1e-10:
       disc=1-4*A; sqrt=math.sqrt(max(0,disc)); r1=...; r2=...; has_root=True
    if not has_root:
       x=0.5*(lox+hix)
       if lox<=hix: ... else x=h0
    else:
       bestx=h0; bestdist=-1
       # left
       l=lox; r=min(hix,r1)
       if l <= r+1e-12:
          mid=0.5*(l+r); if l>r: mid=l
          if abs(mid-h0)>bestdist: bestx=mid; bestdist=abs(...)
       l=max(lox,r2); r=hix
       if l <= r+1e-12:
          mid=...
       # if no piece (bestdist<0) x=h0
       x=bestx
    # ensure x not equal h0? if close return
    return (float(np.clip(x,0,1)), start, a, end, True)
```

Top-level helper signature:
```
def feasible_1d(L, L_low, pos_inv, pos_ca, neg_inv, neg_ca, has_pos, has_neg, h0, center_old):
   if L < L_low - 1e-10: return False
   lox=0.0; hix=1.0
   if has_pos:
      v=h0+float(np.min(L*pos_inv - pos_ca))
      if v<hix: hix=v
   if has_neg:
      v=h0+float(np.max(L*neg_inv - neg_ca))
      if v>lox: lox=v
   if lox > hix + 1e-9: return False
   A=L-center_old+h0-h0*h0
   if A < -1e-9: return False
   if A < 0.25 - 1e-9:
      disc=1.0-4.0*A
      if disc < 0: disc=0.0
      root=math.sqrt(disc)
      r1=0.5*(1-root); r2=0.5*(1+root)
      if lox > r1 + 1e-9 and hix < r2 - 1e-9:
         return False
   return True
```
`center_old` always valid. L_low outside/zero. Tolerance in L comparison: if L slightly below L_low due bisection? feasible false. Good.

Projection: L2 bisection. Let's implement efficient:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-12:
       # fix tiny? maybe distribute? return
       return h
    lo=float(h.min()) - 1e-9
    hi=float(h.max()) + 1e-9
    # We need find t such that sum clip(h-t,0,1)=target.
    # If target > sum(h) need t below min maybe negative? But h sum maybe off after clipping. If target > n (sum all ones) impossible; but target n/2.
    for _ in range(50):
       t=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h-t,0,1))
       if s > target: lo=t
       else: hi=t
    t=0.5*(lo+hi)
    h=np.clip(h-t,0,1)
    # due bisection residual, adjust tiny by scaling?
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-9:
       h += diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       # if still off, use previous iterative?
    return h
```
If h min=max and target not equal? But target within [0,n], t outside; bisection with lo=min-1,hi=max+1. For target half, okay.
Sum residual after threshold not exact if bisection too coarse; 50 gives exact. The `h += diff/n` may break bounds if large; but residual tiny. If not, do a few projection? Better: after t, if residual >1e-8, set t adjust? Use previous iterative to fix? Or compute exact t by bisection more. 50 enough for double.

But if all h values 0 or1 and target fractional (odd n), threshold t in (0,1) gives 0.5 for some clipped? Actually h-t for h=1 ->1-t, for h=0 -> -t =>0, sum = count1*(1-t). To reach half, t such that portion one values 0/1; threshold projection yields some one values fractional exactly? If h discrete 0/1, t sets all ones to 1-t, sum floor*(1-t). It can match target if t=1-target/floor. It's not the L2 closest? It projects by subtracting t from all positive above t; yes. For even target integer, returns unchanged if sum target.

Alternative simpler `project` previous iterative room might be more stable for sum small. But threshold is good.

Now `linear_weighted_gradient` with W possibly zero. If W contains negative? L2 cn nonnegative; lse positive. Good.
```
def linear_weighted_gradient(c,h,n,W):
   L=2*n-1
   if float(np.sum(W)) <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
   x = 1.0 - h
   cr1 = np.correlate(W, x[::-1], mode='full')
   term1 = cr1[n-1:2*n-1]
   cr2 = np.correlate(W, h, mode='full')
   term2 = cr2[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
   return term1 - term2
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def corr(h): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
def compute_c5(h,n): return float(np.max(corr(h)) * (2.0/n))
```
Computing corr twice in compute; in run we can compute c. Maybe `compute_c5_c(c,n)`.

```
def c5_from_c(c,n): return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

Project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy(),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    diff=h.sum()-target
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: return h
    lo=float(h.min())-1.0
    hi=float(h.max())+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       t=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.sum(np.clip(h-t,0,1))
       if s>target: lo=t
       else: hi=t
    t=0.5*(lo+hi)
    h=np.clip(h-t,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-8:
       # fallback iterative room
       for _ in range(100):
           diff=target-h.sum()
           if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
           if diff>0:
              room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
              if tot<1e-15: break
              h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*room,0,1)
           else:
              tot=h.sum()
              if tot<1e-15: break
              h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*h,0,1)
    return h
```
The fallback may be used if threshold due all values at bounds and target exactly? But if h binary sum off by large, threshold should work. Fallback okay.

Interpolate:
```
def interpolate_h(h,n_new):
   n=len(h)
   if n==n_new: return project_feasible(h.copy(),n_new)
   x1=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   x2=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)
   return project_feasible(np.interp(x2,x1,h),n_new)
```

Feasible helper as above.

Solve:
```
def solve_1d(c,h,p,n):
    h0=h[p]
    r1start=p
    r2start=n-1-p
    start = p if p < r2start else r2start
    end = max(n-1+p, 2*n-2-p)
    seg_len=end-start+1
    a=np.zeros(seg_len)
    rel1=p-start
    a[rel1:rel1+n] += 1.0 - h[::-1]
    rel2=r2start-start
    a[rel2:rel2+n] -= h
    outside=0.0
    if start>0: outside=float(np.max(c[:start]))
    if end+1 < c.size:
       v=float(np.max(c[end+1:]))
       if v>outside: outside=v
    cseg=c[start:end+1]
    L_high=float(np.max(cseg))
    if outside>L_high: L_high=outside
    center_rel=n-1-start
    a_solve=a.copy()
    a_solve[center_rel]=0.0
    zero=np.abs(a_solve)<=1e-13
    L_low=outside
    if np.any(zero):
       vz=float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
       if vz>L_low: L_low=vz
    pos=a_solve>1e-13
    neg=a_solve<-1e-13
    has_pos=bool(np.any(pos)); has_neg=bool(np.any(neg))
    if has_pos:
       cpos=cseg[pos]; apos=a_solve[pos]
       pos_inv=1.0/apos; pos_ca=cpos*pos_inv
    else: pos_inv=None; pos_ca=None
    if has_neg:
       cneg=cseg[neg]; aneg=a_solve[neg]
       neg_inv=1.0/aneg; neg_ca=cneg*neg_inv
    else: neg_inv=None; neg_ca=None
    if L_high <= L_low + 1e-12:
       return (h0,start,a,False)
    center_old=float(c[n-1])
    lo=L_low; hi=L_high
    for _ in range(25):
       if hi-lo < 1e-10:
          break
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if feasible_1d(mid,L_low,pos_inv,pos_ca,neg_inv,neg_ca,has_pos,has_neg,h0,center_old): hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    if hi >= L_high - 1e-8:
       return (h0,start,a,False)
    L=hi+1e-8
    lox=0.0; hix=1.0
    if has_pos:
       v=h0+float(np.min(L*pos_inv - pos_ca))
       if v<hix: hix=v
    if has_neg:
       v=h0+float(np.max(L*neg_inv - neg_ca))
       if v>lox: lox=v
    if lox<0: lox=0
    if hix>1: hix=1
    A=L-center_old+h0-h0*h0
    if A < -1e-7:
       return (h0,start,a,False)
    if A < 0.25 - 1e-12:
       disc=1.0-4.0*A
       if disc<0: disc=0.0
       root=math.sqrt(disc)
       r1=0.5*(1.0-root); r2=0.5*(1.0+root)
       bestx=h0; bestdist=-1.0
       # left
       l=lox; rr=min(hix,r1)
       if l <= rr + 1e-12:
           mid=0.5*(l+rr)
           if abs(mid-h0)>bestdist: bestx=mid; bestdist=abs(mid-h0)
       l=max(lox,r2); rr=hix
       if l <= rr + 1e-12:
           mid=0.5*(l+rr)
           if abs(mid-h0)>bestdist: bestx=mid; bestdist=abs(mid-h0)
       x=bestx
    else:
       x=0.5*(lox+hix)
    if x<0: x=0
    if x>1: x=1
    if abs(x-h0)<1e-11:
       return (h0,start,a,False)
    return (x,start,a,True)
```
Tolerance `hi >= L_high - 1e-8`: If L_high ~100, 1e-8 counts C 3e-11; okay. But if improvements are only 1e-9, skip. Fine.

Potential issue: If `L_low` = `outside` and `hi` = outside (no reduction relative to affected but outside max equals L_high?) L_high outside if affected max ≤outside. Skip. Good.

If current local max is cseg (affected) and can reduce to outside lower, L_high>cseg? hi lower by maybe >1e-8, update.

Need if `pos_inv` arrays length large; `L*pos_inv - pos_ca` allocates. Good.

`cd_local`:
```
def cd_local(h,n,max_passes,deadline,frac=1.0,alpha=20.0):
    h=project_feasible(np.array(h,dtype=float).copy(),n)
    c=corr(h)
    best_c=c5_from_c(c,n); best_h=h.copy()
    if frac < 1.0:
       nsel=int(round(n*frac))
       nsel=max(1,min(n,nsel))
    else: nsel=n
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       if frac < 1.0:
          cn=c*(2.0/n)
          m=float(np.max(cn))
          w=np.exp(alpha*(cn-m))
          sw=float(np.sum(w))
          if sw <=0:
             w=np.zeros(cn.size); w[np.argmax(cn)]=1.0
          else: w=w/sw
          g=linear_weighted_gradient(c,h,n,w)
          if nsel < n:
             abs_g=np.abs(g)
             # use argpartition
             idx=np.argpartition(abs_g, n-nsel)[n-nsel:]
             idx=idx[np.argsort(abs_g[idx])[::-1]]
          else:
             idx=np.arange(n)
       else:
          idx=np.arange(n) if (it%2==0) else np.arange(n-1,-1,-1)
       moved=0
       for p in idx:
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          sol=solve_1d(c,h,p,n)
          if sol is None: continue
          x,pstart,ause,use_center=sol
          old=h[p]
          if abs(x-old)>1e-11:
             delta=x-old
             c[pstart:pstart+ause.size] += delta*ause
             if use_center:
                c[n-1] -= delta*delta
             h[p]=x
             moved += 1
       if moved==0:
          break
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
       c=corr(h)
       cur=c5_from_c(c,n)
       if cur < best_c:
          best_c=cur
          best_h=h.copy()
       # optional rollback if cur > best_c + 0.005? no
    return best_h,best_c
```
If frac<1 and nsel=n? argpartition kth n-nsel invalid if nsel=n; handled else.
`np.argpartition(abs_g, n-nsel)[n-nsel:]` when nsel=1 kth=n-1 gives top1. Good.
Sorting idx by abs_g descending costs nsel log n; okay. For nsel maybe 150, sort small. Could use `np.sort(idx)[::-1]` but need by abs_g.
Maybe active gradient stale; okay.

What about objective after projection if worse; we don't rollback. But best_h kept. However cd_local continues from worse h, may waste. Add small rollback if cur > best_c + 0.001? But it might be necessary to escape? Since each pass before projection nonincreases, the increase is due projection. If projection worsens significantly, maybe due many variable updates accumulating? Projection shifts all to satisfy sum, but sum changes by sum of deltas. The shift might be significant if deltas coherent. Could cause objective increase. If we rollback to best, we may lose shape. But best is only slightly better? Hard. I think no rollback, but we can use h=best_h if cur > best_c + 1e-3 to avoid compounding. Let's add:
```
if cur > best_c + 0.002:
    h=best_h.copy(); c=corr(h)
```
0.002 C tolerance. Could hinder if exploring flat; but active CD doesn't explore. Perturb phases handle. Good.

But if best_c remains old and current h slightly worse, continuing may find better; rollback might discard new landscape. Maybe use no rollback. Since exact variable updates nonincrease max, projection only small; likely cur ≤ best_c + small. Keep no rollback.

Now no FW/SLSQP. Is this enough? Multi-n and active may improve. We might need global search. Let's add a simple deterministic perturbation plus active CD in run.

`run` sequence:
```
def run(...):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-15
 try: h_best=...; n_best=len...
 except: n_best=500; h_best=np.full(n_best,0.5)
 if n_best>1000: h_best=interpolate_h(h_best,800); n_best=800
 h_best=project_feasible(...)
 best_c=compute...
 # baseline full/active
 if time < deadline-60:
   h1,c1=cd_local(h_best,n_best,6,deadline,frac=1.0)
   if c1<best_c: update
   h1,c1=cd_local(h1,n_best,20,deadline,frac=0.25)
   if c1<best_c: update
 # n candidates
 n_set=[]
 base=n_best
 for nn in [350,400,450,500,550,600,650,700,800]: if even and nn<=1000: add
 # include base if not, order base first, then others by distance to base? Maybe small first?
 candidates=sorted(set([base]+n_set))
 # reorder: base, then smaller than base ascending, then greater descending? 
 order=[base]
 order += [x for x in candidates if x<base][::-1] # smaller first? maybe 350,400...
 order += [x for x in candidates if x>base]
 # limit to 6: if len order>6, maybe keep close? Use all with time.
 for idx,n_try in enumerate(order):
    if time.time() > deadline-80: break
    if idx==0: continue
    h_int=interpolate_h(h_best,n_try)
    cur_c=compute_c5(h_int,n_try)
    # phases depending size
    if n_try <=450:
        h_c,c=c_local(h_int,n_try,40,deadline,frac=0.3)
        h_c,c=c_local(h_c,n_try,5,deadline,frac=1.0)
    elif n_try <=650:
        h_c,c=cd_local(h_int,n_try,25,deadline,frac=0.25)
        h_c,c=cd_local(h_c,n_try,4,deadline,frac=1.0)
    else:
        h_c,c=cd_local(h_int,n_try,15,deadline,frac=0.25)
        h_c,c=cd_local(h_c,n_try,3,deadline,frac=1.0)
    if c<best_c: update
    # if updated, maybe active more
    if c<best_c and time<deadline-100:
        h_a,c_a=cd_local(h_best,n_best,10,deadline,frac=0.3)
        if c_a<best_c: update
 # perturbation on current best
 scales=[0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1]
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 for s in scales:
   if time.time()>deadline-80: break
   h_p=project_feasible(h_best + rng.normal(0,s,n_best),n_best)
   h_c,c=cd_local(h_p,n_best,15,deadline,frac=0.3)
   h_c,c=cd_local(h_c,n_best,3,deadline,frac=1.0)
   if c<best_c: update
 # final polish
 if time.time()<deadline-5:
   h_f,c_f=cd_local(h_best,n_best,8,deadline,frac=0.3)
   h_f,c_f=cd_local(h_f,n_best,3,deadline,frac=1.0)
   if c_f<best_c: update
 h_best=project_feasible(h_best,len(h_best))
 best_c=c5_from_c(corr(h_best),len(h_best))
 return h_best,best_c,len(h_best)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time


def erdos_corr(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5_from_corr(c, n):
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.array(h, dtype=float).copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) < 1e-12:
        return h

    lo = float(h.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(h.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(h - t, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = t
        else:
            hi = t
    t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(h - t, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-9:
        for _ in range(100):
            diff = target - float(h.sum())
            if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
                break
            if diff > 0.0:
                room = 1.0 - h
                tot = float(room.sum())
                if tot < 1e-15:
                    break
                h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
            else:
                tot = float(h.sum())
                if tot < 1e-15:
                    break
                h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def interpolate_h(h, n_new):
    n = len(h)
    if n == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x2 = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x2, x1, h), n_new)


def feasible_1d(L, L_low, pos_inv, pos_ca, neg_inv, neg_ca,
                has_pos, has_neg, h0, center_old):
    if L < L_low - 1e-10:
        return False
    lox = 0.0
    hix = 1.0
    if has_pos:
        v = h0 + float(np.min(L * pos_inv - pos_ca))
        if v < hix:
            hix = v
    if has_neg:
        v = h0 + float(np.max(L * neg_inv - neg_ca))
        if v > lox:
            lox = v
    if lox > hix + 1e-9:
        return False

    A = L - center_old + h0 - h0 * h0
    if A < -1e-9:
        return False
    if A < 0.25 - 1e-12:
        disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * A
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        root = math.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - root)
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + root)
        if lox > r1 + 1e-9 and hix < r2 - 1e-9:
            return False
    return True


def solve_1d(c, h, p, n):
    h0 = h[p]

    s1 = p
    s2 = n - 1 - p
    start = s1 if s1 < s2 else s2
    end = n - 1 + p
    v = 2 * n - 2 - p
    if v > end:
        end = v

    seg_len = end - start + 1
    a = np.zeros(seg_len, dtype=float)

    rel1 = p - start
    a[rel1:rel1 + n] += 1.0
    a[rel1:rel1 + n] -= h[::-1]

    rel2 = s2 - start
    a[rel2:rel2 + n] -= h

    outside = 0.0
    if start > 0:
        outside = float(np.max(c[:start]))
    if end + 1 < c.size:
        v = float(np.max(c[end + 1:]))
        if v > outside:
            outside = v

    cseg = c[start:end + 1]
    L_high = float(np.max(cseg))
    if outside > L_high:
        L_high = outside

    center_rel = n - 1 - start
    pos = a > 1e-13
    neg = a < -1e-13
    zero = ~(pos | neg)
    pos[center_rel] = False
    neg[center_rel] = False
    zero[center_rel] = False

    L_low = outside
    if np.any(zero):
        vz = float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
        if vz > L_low:
            L_low = vz

    has_pos = bool(np.any(pos))
    has_neg = bool(np.any(neg))

    if has_pos:
        cpos = cseg[pos]
        apos = a[pos]
        pos_inv = 1.0 / apos
        pos_ca = cpos * pos_inv
    else:
        pos_inv = None
        pos_ca = None

    if has_neg:
        cneg = cseg[neg]
        aneg = a[neg]
        neg_inv = 1.0 / aneg
        neg_ca = cneg * neg_inv
    else:
        neg_inv = None
        neg_ca = None

    if L_high <= L_low + 1e-12:
        return (h0, start, a, False)

    center_old = float(c[n - 1])
    lo = L_low
    hi = L_high

    for _ in range(25):
        if hi - lo < 1e-10:
            break
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if feasible_1d(mid, L_low, pos_inv, pos_ca, neg_inv, neg_ca,
                       has_pos, has_neg, h0, center_old):
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    if hi >= L_high - 1e-8:
        return (h0, start, a, False)

    L = hi + 1e-8
    lox = 0.0
    hix = 1.0
    if has_pos:
        v = h0 + float(np.min(L * pos_inv - pos_ca))
        if v < hix:
            hix = v
    if has_neg:
        v = h0 + float(np.max(L * neg_inv - neg_ca))
        if v > lox:
            lox = v

    if lox < 0.0:
        lox = 0.0
    if hix > 1.0:
        hix = 1.0
    if lox > hix:
        return (h0, start, a, False)

    A = L - center_old + h0 - h0 * h0
    if A < -1e-7:
        return (h0, start, a, False)

    if A < 0.25 - 1e-12:
        disc = 1.0 - 4.0 * A
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        root = math.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - root)
        r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + root)

        bestx = h0
        bestdist = -1.0

        l = lox
        rr = min(hix, r1)
        if l <= rr + 1e-12:
            mid = 0.5 * (l + rr)
            d = abs(mid - h0)
            if d > bestdist:
                bestx = mid
                bestdist = d

        l = max(lox, r2)
        rr = hix
        if l <= rr + 1e-12:
            mid = 0.5 * (l + rr)
            d = abs(mid - h0)
            if d > bestdist:
                bestx = mid
                bestdist = d

        x = bestx
    else:
        x = 0.5 * (lox + hix)

    if x < 0.0:
        x = 0.0
    elif x > 1.0:
        x = 1.0

    if abs(x - h0) < 1e-11:
        return (h0, start, a, False)
    return (float(x), start, a, True)


def linear_weighted_gradient(c, h, n, w):
    if float(np.sum(w)) <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    xrev = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    cr1 = np.correlate(w, xrev, mode="full")
    term1 = cr1[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    cr2 = np.correlate(w, h, mode="full")
    term2 = cr2[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]

    return term1 - term2


def cd_local(h, n, max_passes, deadline, frac=1.0, alpha=20.0):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    c = erdos_corr(h)
    best_c = c5_from_corr(c, n)
    best_h = h.copy()

    nsel = n
    if frac < 1.0:
        nsel = max(1, min(n, int(round(n * frac))))

    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        if frac < 1.0 and nsel < n:
            cn = c * (2.0 / n)
            m = float(np.max(cn))
            w = np.exp(alpha * (cn - m))
            sw = float(np.sum(w))
            if sw <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(cn.size, dtype=float)
                w[int(np.argmax(cn))] = 1.0
            else:
                w /= sw

            g = linear_weighted_gradient(c, h, n, w)
            ag = np.abs(g)
            idx = np.argpartition(ag, n - nsel)[n - nsel:]
            idx = idx[np.argsort(ag[idx])[::-1]]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(n) if (it % 2 == 0) else np.arange(n - 1, -1, -1)

        moved = 0
        for p in idx:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            x, pstart, ause, use_center = solve_1d(c, h, p, n)
            old = h[p]
            if abs(x - old) > 1e-11:
                delta = x - old
                c[pstart:pstart + ause.size] += delta * ause
                if use_center:
                    c[n - 1] -= delta * delta
                h[p] = x
                moved += 1

        if moved == 0:
            break

        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = erdos_corr(h)
        cur = c5_from_corr(c, n)
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 18.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h_best = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_best = len(h_best)
    except Exception:
        n_best = 500
        h_best = np.full(n_best, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if n_best > 1000:
        h_best = interpolate_h(h_best, 800)
        n_best = 800

    h_best = project_feasible(h_best, n_best)
    best_c = c5_from_corr(erdos_corr(h_best), n_best)

    if time.time() < deadline - 90:
        h1, c1 = cd_local(h_best, n_best, 3, deadline, frac=1.0)
        if c1 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h1, c1
        h1, c1 = cd_local(h_best, n_best, 22, deadline, frac=0.30, alpha=20.0)
        if c1 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h1, c1
        h1, c1 = cd_local(h_best, n_best, 4, deadline, frac=1.0)
        if c1 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h1, c1

    candidates = [350, 400, 450, 500, 550, 600, 650, 700, 800]
    seen = set()
    ordered = []
    for nn in candidates:
        if nn % 2 == 0 and nn <= 1000 and nn not in seen and nn != n_best:
            seen.add(nn)
            ordered.append(nn)

    for n_try in ordered:
        if time.time() > deadline - 75:
            break

        h_cur = interpolate_h(h_best, n_try)
        cur_c = c5_from_corr(erdos_corr(h_cur), n_try)

        if n_try <= 400:
            act, full = 55, 6
        elif n_try <= 550:
            act, full = 38, 5
        elif n_try <= 700:
            act, full = 24, 4
        else:
            act, full = 16, 3

        if time.time() < deadline - 70:
            h1, c1 = cd_local(h_cur, n_try, act, deadline, frac=0.28, alpha=20.0)
            if c1 < cur_c:
                h_cur, cur_c = h1, c1

        if time.time() < deadline - 60:
            h1, c1 = cd_local(h_cur, n_try, max(4, act // 4), deadline,
                              frac=0.12, alpha=6.0)
            if c1 < cur_c:
                h_cur, cur_c = h1, c1

        if time.time() < deadline - 50:
            h1, c1 = cd_local(h_cur, n_try, full, deadline, frac=1.0)
            if c1 < cur_c:
                h_cur, cur_c = h1, c1

        if cur_c < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h_cur, cur_c

    for s in (0.01, 0.025, 0.06, 0.12):
        if time.time() > deadline - 70:
            break
        hp = project_feasible(h_best + rng.normal(0.0, s, n_best), n_best)
        h1, c1 = cd_local(hp, n_best, 16, deadline, frac=0.30, alpha=18.0)
        if c1 < cur_c if False else True:
            h2, c2 = cd_local(h1, n_best, 3, deadline, frac=1.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                h_best, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        h1, c1 = cd_local(h_best, n_best, 10, deadline, frac=0.30, alpha=20.0)
        if c1 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
        h1, c1 = cd_local(h_best, n_best, 4, deadline, frac=1.0)
        if c1 < best_c:
            h_best, best_c = h1, c1

    h_best = project_feasible(h_best, len(h_best))
    best_c = c5_from_corr(erdos_corr(h_best), len(h_best))
    return h_best, best_c, len(h_best)
```
